- **ratchet-adversary**: Ratchet adversarial reviewer. Assumes the checkpoint's code is wrong and tries to prove it — hunting for violations of the project's written standards (lens=standards) and for bugs, edge cases, security and data-integrity problems (lens=correctness), or both. Every finding cites a rule or a concrete failure scenario. Approves only by returning zero findings. Never fixes code. Spawned fresh each round by the ratchet-adversarial-loop. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-adversary.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-app-scout**: Ratchet loop scout. Checks the current state of the app before a build — detects the stack, test runner, start command and URL, makes sure the app actually runs, finds login credentials/seed data for UI captures, and drafts .ratchet/standards.md from the codebase if no written standard exists. Spawned by the ratchet-orchestrator in parallel with the checkpoint planner. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-app-scout.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-behavior-reviewer**: Ratchet UI-gate behaviour reviewer. Compares how the real app and the reference respond to the same interaction journey — step-by-step screenshots, accessibility trees, console errors and browser-only test cases — and lists every behavioural difference (wrong result, missing feedback, broken focus, validation, navigation, loading/empty/error states). Returns INVALID when the starting states don't match. Works only from an isolated INPUT.md folder; run by ui_review.py through a provider other than the host when one is available, otherwise as the host's own sub-agent. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-behavior-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-checkpoint-planner**: Ratchet checkpoint planner. Splits a large feature, screen migration or refactor into small ordered checkpoints of increasing complexity, each with plain-language "done when" criteria and a decision on which gates apply, written to checkpoints.json. PLAN mode creates the plan; EXTEND mode appends checkpoints from human feedback. Returns QUESTIONS instead of guessing when the request is ambiguous. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-checkpoint-planner.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-critic**: HARNESS council critic — the ratchet. For each CONFIRMED root, it chooses the cheapest DETERMINISTIC output (new guardrail/ESLint Tier-1 · new pre-edit lesson Tier-2 · prune-revision of an existing lesson · a CLAUDE.md pointer proposal). If no deterministic output exists, it records "no actionable artifact + reason" — never inventing a change. It proposes; the librarian enacts. Read-only. ([SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz](https://github.com/SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ratchet-critic.md) · ★2)
- **ratchet-design-system**: Ratchet design-system agent. Creates or updates DESIGN.md — exact design tokens, component specs with every state, layout, content and accessibility rules, and an implementation map to real files — by extracting from the existing codebase or building from a brief. Follows the ratchet-design-md skill. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator before prototyping when no DESIGN.md exists, or directly when the user wants a design system file. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-design-system.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-engine**: Update thresholds, generate rules, record decisions, and update memory after each audit cycle. - **ratchet-fixer**: Ratchet fixer. - **ratchet-implementer**: Ratchet implementer. - **ratchet-maintainer**: Impedir que erros recorrentes se repitam. ([ratazio/sdd-harness-guardian](https://github.com/ratazio/sdd-harness-guardian/blob/HEAD/.harness/agents/ratchet-maintainer.md) · ★1)
- **ratchet-prototyper**: Ratchet design/prototype agent. - **ratchet-test-planner**: Ratchet test planner. - **ratchet-test-runner**: Ratchet test runner. - **ratchet-test-writer**: Ratchet test writer. Turns a checkpoint's planned test cases (tests/CPn.json) into real, runnable tests tagged with their case IDs, written BEFORE the implementation so every one can run and at least one fails on an assertion. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator at the start of each checkpoint (sometimes several in parallel on different files). ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-test-writer.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-variant-writer**: Ratchet variant writer. - **rb-auditor**: Blind auditor. Read-only: verdict per criterion is pass, fail or not-observable with evidence; any fail is a kickback. Dispatched by /rb-build and /rb-fix for tier B and C, and by the optional PR audit routine. Dispatched by /rb-verify, and again to fix tests rb-healer classified as test faults. Writes only under e2e/ and the Playwright config; never touches app code, never runs git or gh in any form. Returns the files written and how it explored. ([ragarciaga/rockbuilt](https://github.com/ragarciaga/rockbuilt/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/rb-e2e-writer.md) · ★1)
- **rb-healer**: Runs a feature's Playwright E2E suite and diagnoses every failure as app-fault, test-fault, environment or flaky, with evidence for each, and audits passing tests for assertions that cannot fail. ([doubts-suplab/eeik-bootstrap](https://github.com/doubts-suplab/eeik-bootstrap/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/rca-agent.md) · ★1)

- **rca-analyst**: Analista de causa raiz que conduz post-mortems e documenta learnings. Foca em aprendizado sistemico, nao em blame. Use este agente para: - Conduzir post-mortems - Documentar RCA (Root Cause Analysis) - Identificar acoes preventivas - Extrair learnings para o time Examples: - &lt;example&gt; Context: Incidente resolvido, precisa de RCA user: "Faca o RCA do incidente INC-20260111-001" assistant: "Vou usar @rca-analyst para conduzir a analise de causa raiz" &lt;commentary&gt; Post-mortems sao essenciais para aprendizado organizacional &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([arbgjr/sdlc_agentico](https://github.com/arbgjr/sdlc_agentico/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/rca-analyst.md) · ★3 · 4 copies)
- **rca-analyst**: Root-cause analysis specialist. Use when something fails, regresses, or behaves unexpectedly — a validation check fails, a skill stops triggering, an eval regresses, CI breaks, or a deliverable misbehaves — and you need the true cause, not a symptom patch. Use PROACTIVELY when a fix attempt fails to hold. Investigates and reports only; never edits. ([kabaka/ai-dlc](https://github.com/kabaka/ai-dlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/rca-analyst.md))
- **rca-debugger**: Root-cause analyzer for complex multi-system failures — the third stage of the debugging escalation chain (build-error-resolver → systematic-debugger → rca-debugger → escalation-fixer). Escalation from systematic-debugger when the bisect is inconclusive, there is a CI-vs-local discrepancy, the bug is flaky, or the regression is cross-layer. Produces a 5-why analysis, a fishbone (Ishikawa) diagram, and a fix proposal. If the recommended fix needs architectural authority, hand off to escalation-fixer. ([sangrokjung/claude-forge](https://github.com/sangrokjung/claude-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/rca-debugger.md) · ★881)
- **rca-doc**: Expert agent for drafting Root Cause Analysis (RCA) documents by gathering information from multiple sources, investigating root causes through pattern matching and similar incident analysis, and creating well-formatted Google Docs.
